# T3 reconciliation ledger: frozen 805-commit inventory

This ledger is the row-level companion to
[the maintained implementation plan](./t3-nightly-reconciliation-20260904.md). It is intentionally
truthful about the current checkpoint: 42 rows are published and merged, 73 retain source ownership
from the historical 27-group map, ten expanded-intake rows are allocated exactly to current PRs 1-2,
two have accepted partial slices (including the merged Group 09 client slice of `c2283ce14`) and
planned follow-up slices, and 678 remain unreviewed pending semantic classification. “Pending” is not
an equivalence or inapplicability claim.

## Inventory proof and provenance

- Repository: `pingdotgg/t3code`.
- Previously audited range: exclusive
  `053affbed2659f90cd1b1efaaa7a75865c4131c7` through inclusive
  `fff33f9e851912363c5b1f3ac65598be35eb5f0d`; GitHub compare reported
  `ahead_by=244` and pagination returned 100 + 100 + 44 rows.
- Newer range: exclusive `fff33f9e851912363c5b1f3ac65598be35eb5f0d` through inclusive
  `82f64cd8d046ab4ee8588f7bcbc1e66a4a0c80fe`; GitHub compare reported
  `ahead_by=224` and pagination returned 100 + 100 + 24 rows.
- Expanded 2026-09-07 intake: 337 locally available non-merge commits, exclusive
  `82f64cd8d046ab4ee8588f7bcbc1e66a4a0c80fe` through inclusive
  `0d34579d674920cc47fc5c908494f51ed3895204`. These are rows 469-805; row 469 is
  `89ee69e4430b21ee14565abf5c34dae43f38c1d8` and row 805 is the new cutoff.
- Combined proof: 805 rows, 805 unique full SHAs. The first row is
  `fc262f1a28d8305951c751f2486da6ca72e6c1d1`; row 244 is
  `fff33f9e851912363c5b1f3ac65598be35eb5f0d`; row 245 is
  `77e35c561259733d880ab62a43aad0894d301d9b`; row 468 is the former cutoff
  `82f64cd8d046ab4ee8588f7bcbc1e66a4a0c80fe`; and row 805 is the frozen head
  `0d34579d674920cc47fc5c908494f51ed3895204`.
- Official Antigravity foundation `06336460c9988f29c71e839c4c9c840c4552e077` is row 239 in the
  previously audited range; it was already inside the frozen universe before the 224 newer commits.
- Source metadata came from the read-only GitHub compare API on 2026-09-04 using
  `per_page=100`, pages 1-3 for each range. No Git ref was fetched or mutated.
- After Group 02 merged, one bounded `--no-tags` fetch made exact frozen cutoff
  `82f64cd8d046ab4ee8588f7bcbc1e66a4a0c80fe` locally readable through `FETCH_HEAD` without moving
  `upstream/main` from `fff33f9e851912363c5b1f3ac65598be35eb5f0d` or extending the ledger.
- All 117 previously named source prefixes remain exact. The ten newly approved PR 1/PR 2 source
  prefixes also resolve exactly once in rows 469-805. One older source, `98a29cbaa`, has approved
  slices in historical Groups 07 and 27.
- Commit-local source paths remain deferred for unclassified rows 1-468. GitHub's compare response
  did not provide a per-commit path set, and the plan requires source tracing before classification
  rather than treating the range-level 300-file truncation as commit evidence. Rows 469-805 instead
  use their locally available commit objects, so their path cells are populated at intake.
- Titles are first-line upstream commit subjects. Authors and author dates in rows 1-468 preserve
  upstream API metadata. Rows 469-805 preserve local Git author identity, strict ISO author date, and
  commit-local touched paths for later source-attributed publication.
- Auxiliary read-only source-context passes now cover all 468 rows. The rows 1-150 note contains 130
  detailed records plus 20 rows intentionally skipped because an approved group already owns them;
  row 17, `8f525af5afa93dff58775030d9b503d3bda36b7b`, is one such Group 17-owned skip, not
  unresolved intake. Rows 201-300 contain 28 approved/published-owner skips, four observations of
  represented current behavior, and 68 other advisory dispositions. Cookie-import rows 277-279 and
  284 remain excluded; rows 223 and 243 are not approved Group 27 or Group 13 intake. Rows 301-400
  are also advisory. Rows 401-468 contain 16 candidate/unverified, 23 approved-owner, and 29
  excluded/inapplicable observations; rows 401-434 split 11/8/15 and rows 435-468 split 5/15/14.
  Among their explicit exclusions, row 418's native Rust monitor and row 456's `ThemeEditorHost` are
  absent, while row 445's automatic post-update continuation is plan-excluded. None of these context
  notes changes a ledger disposition until its evidence is promoted into the exact row. Three
  additional bounded passes covered rows 469-805 in 135/94/108-row segments. Their provisional
  adapt/equivalent/inapplicable/skip counts were 20/12/18/85, 36/10/11/37, and 61/1/11/35. Those
  classifications are superseded by the approved 17-PR map and are not final row dispositions.

## Ledger completion rule

Before final closeout, every pending path, group, disposition, evidence, dependency/gate, and fork
commit/PR cell must be resolved. A conditional or applicable-slice row requires a reproduction or
boundary proof. No row may be closed from its title alone. The final counts must still total 805 unique
SHAs, and anything after frozen head `0d34579d674920cc47fc5c908494f51ed3895204` must be reported as a
separate unapproved range.
